[jart] now we see that 1.4554 seconds later, confirmation is received
       via schwab's websocket that the order for leg #2 has been
       created. in this confirmation, schwab claims the nbbo bid is
       currently at $126.10 even though databento says it's now at
       ~$124. this 126.10 number appears to be made up. my hunch is that
       schwab leaked my order data to the person who owned the quote,
       they pulled their bid by a few dollars. then schwab made up a
       fictitious number in-between that's an improvement over the new
       nbbo, so they could argue yeah our systems made that shit up, but
       it's made up in a way that's favorable to you! except it's not
       favorable to me at all. because the bid was actually $126.70 and
       had sat at that price for a whole second until the instant I
       talked to Schwab.

       that my order for leg #2 has been routed to DASH. I assume since
       DASH publicly states they don't do PFOF, that this is how Schwab
       routes orders to CBOE i.e. the actual one and only SPX exchange.
       none of their PFOF partners (e.g. Jane Street) wanted to fill my
       order on leg #2 so Schwab sends it to DASH as a last resort. I'm
       quite confident this is what DASH means. I know with Alpaca they
       have a DMA feature for Elite Smart Router users, which lets you
       post orders directly to lit exchanges, and Alpaca says that they
       use DASH as their partner for doing it.
